| *(this commit)* | `0bab0157` *(pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Refactor (one create path): the headless server's deferred workspace/tab creates stop calling the direct `App::create_*` helpers and dispatch `workspace.create` / `tab.create` through the same API handler a socket client hits, so the last server-side mutation bypass is closed.** Fifth link of the M5 chain, on top of the `9a91a2ac` dispatch seam. **Mechanism:** the eleven inline deferred-request blocks in `run` (`src/server/headless.rs:441`) are extracted whole into `handle_deferred_requests_headless` (`:547`), which returns one `bool` the caller turns into `needs_render` + `needs_full_render`; inside it, `request_new_workspace` and `request_new_tab` now build `Method::WorkspaceCreate` / `Method::TabCreate` (the tab create taking `requested_new_tab_name` as `label`) and `request_new_workspace_cwd` builds `WorkspaceCreate { cwd: Some(..) }`, each through the new `dispatch_headless_runtime_mutation` (`:681`) which pushes an `api::ApiRequestMessage` over a local `mpsc` channel, waits 5 s, and maps a decodable `ErrorResponse` to `Err(ErrorBody)` — so a failure is logged with the API `code`/`message` instead of an `io::Error`, and the cwd failure still falls back to `Mode::Navigate`. `handle_api_request_with_shutdown_check` (`:2578`) becomes a thin wrapper over the new `handle_api_request_with_shutdown_check_inner` (`:2582`), whose `skip_default_workspace_for_request` parameter ORs into the existing `ServerStop`/`ServerLiveHandoff` skip (`:2648`): a server-initiated create IS the workspace creation, so the post-request `ensure_default_workspace()` fallback must not race it or paper over a rejected create. With the headless loop off them, the direct-mutation helpers have no production caller left and are gated `#[cfg(test)]` as characterization targets — `App::create_workspace` (`src/app/creation.rs:60`), `create_workspace_with_events` (`:75`), `create_tab` (`:86`), `create_tab_with_options` (`:131`), the `tracing::error` import that only they use (`:3`), and `crate::logging::tab_created` (`src/logging.rs:271`), whose only caller was `create_tab_with_options`. Now that the headless path shares the seam, the `tui`-scoped names lose the qualifier: `dispatch_tui_api_request` -> `dispatch_api_request` and `dispatch_tui_deferred_api_request` -> `dispatch_deferred_api_request` (`src/app/api.rs:28`, `:39`), `dispatch_tui_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_runtime_mutation` and `dispatch_tui_deferred_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_deferred_runtime_mutation` (`src/app/runtime_mutations.rs:6`, `:10`), renamed across all 21 call sites (7 in `src/app/input/modal.rs`, 10 in `src/app/input/navigate.rs`, 3 in `src/app/mod.rs`, 3 in `src/app/worktrees.rs`, 6 inside `runtime_mutations.rs` itself). The `9a91a2ac` hold-out ends: `tui.server.reload_config` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:327`) moves from the raw API entry point onto `dispatch_runtime_mutation` like every other TUI action. **Behaviour change carried from upstream:** a deferred `tab.create` with no active workspace is now REJECTED by `handle_tab_create` (`workspace_not_found`) where `App::create_tab` used to silently fall back to creating a whole workspace; the new guard test pins that the rejection is not then masked by `ensure_default_workspace()`. **Adaptations vs upstream:** (1) every upstream `env: Default::default()` on `WorkspaceCreateParams` / `TabCreateParams` is DROPPED — those structs have no `env` field in this fork (`src/api/schema/{workspaces,tabs}.rs`), consistent with the same adaptation recorded on the `1a4e94e5` row; (2) the `+334` `src/server/headless.rs` rewrite is re-anchored BY HAND around the fork's DB-worker install rather than applied mechanically, because a mechanical apply can drop the `install_db_workers` call and still compile (the field is an `Option`), with the only symptom every `zynk.message_received` answering `receipt_worker_unavailable`; (3) one fork-only test is added beyond the upstream two (see below), so this file's diff is `+375` rather than `+334`. **Invariants checked:** the fork's receipt + embedding worker install is intact — `install_db_workers` is still DEFINED at `src/server/headless.rs:3924` and still CALLED from the server startup path `run_server` (`:3792`), from `restore_db_workers_after_failed_handoff` (`:1143`) and from the handoff import server (`:4001`), and the receipt suites prove it live (`-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52); `src/app/creation.rs` gains only the five `#[cfg(test)]` lines, so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (32 lines, sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59…` before and after); `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs` and `src/ui.rs` are untouched, so `AppState`/`PaneState` stay pure data (97 `AppState::test_new()` and 343 `Workspace::test_new(` sites still compile) and `render(&AppState, frame)` gained no mutation; `src/app/actions.rs` is untouched, so the `AppEvent::HookStateReported` identity region is trivially byte-identical (sha256 `145b9ecd18ed1b50…`); fork-owned `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` are untouched (0 changed files); the `Method` wire-id list is unchanged (`method_wire_ids_are_stable` green, same 80 ids) and this commit adds no method and no event kind. **Tests (RED first):** the two mirrored upstream tests `headless_deferred_workspace_create_uses_runtime_events` (`src/server/headless.rs:4199`) and `headless_deferred_named_tab_create_uses_runtime_events` (`:4223`) are PARITY tests, not RED ones — run against the pure extraction with the old `create_workspace()`/`create_tab()` bodies still in place they PASS (2/2), which is the point: the refactor must not change the emitted `WorkspaceCreated` -> `TabCreated` -> `PaneCreated` sequence or the `label` on the tab. The genuinely new production behaviour is the skip flag, so a fork-only guard test covers it: `headless_deferred_create_failure_does_not_seed_a_default_workspace` (`:4265`) connects a full app client, leaves `Mode::Onboarding` for `Navigate` so the fallback is live, fires `request_new_tab` with no workspaces, and asserts the rejected create leaves `workspaces` empty with zero terminal runtimes. RED proven by flipping the new argument to `false`: `panicked at src/server/headless.rs:4291: a rejected deferred create must not fall through to ensure_default_workspace`, green with `true`. Two upstream test helpers land with them — `test_headless_server_with_event_hub` (`:4070`, `test_headless_server` now delegates to it) and `shutdown_test_runtimes` (`:4122`, drains `terminal_runtimes` so a test that spawns a real PTY does not leak it). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0 with no orphaned dead code; `--bin zynk -E 'test(/server::headless/)'` 90/90 (87 -> 90), `-E 'test(/input::modal/) | test(/input::navigate/) | test(/worktrees/) | test(/runtime_mutations/)'` 122/122, `-E 'test(/api::schema/)'` 32/32, `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28, `-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `-E 'test(/app::/)'` 610/610; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test multi_client --test detach_reattach --test client_mode --test api_ping --test cross_area` 88/88; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2724/2724 passed, 1 skipped (2721 -> 2724, +3 = the three new tests); per-file `#[test]`+`#[tokio::test]` counts show no decrease (`server/headless.rs` 87 -> 90, all eight other touched files unchanged) and `src/` totals 2722 -> 2725; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks); `just check` green end to end — 3042/3042 passed, 2 skipped, plus the 64 maintenance unittests OK. One full-suite run hit the known load-dependent flake `tests/zynk_query.rs::hybrid_rrf_end_to_end` (`vector_index.ready=false` with `pending_jobs: 1` — the embedding queue had not drained inside the deadline); it passed 3/3 in isolation and the next full `just check` was clean, and this commit touches no file under `src/zynk/**`. |
| *(this commit)* | *(none — fork-only test harness fix)* | **Test-only (harness race): the `wait_for_file` test helper waits for the file to be NON-EMPTY, not merely readable, so a poll that lands between an external writer's create and its write no longer returns `""` and fails the caller's content assertion.** No production line changes. **The race:** the fork's `#[cfg(test)] fn wait_for_file` (`src/app/input/mod.rs:657`) polled `std::fs::read_to_string` every 20 ms for up to 2 s and returned the FIRST `Ok(_)` — including `Ok("")`. Its five callers all drive an external writer that creates the destination and then fills it: `terminal_direct_edit_scrollback_opens_editor_pane` (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:852`) and `edit_scrollback_key_opens_focused_runtime_scrollback_in_editor_pane` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2787`) set `EDITOR` to `sh -c 'cp "$1" <out>' sh`, and `cp` opens the destination with `O_CREAT` + `O_TRUNC` before writing it, so between those two syscalls the path exists with zero length; `direct_custom_command_runs_before_forwarding_to_pane` (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:891`), `custom_command_runs_from_prefix_key_in_navigate_mode` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2642`) and `pane_overlay_command_opens_and_closes_after_exit` (`:2718`) drive a `printf > <out>` redirect with the same create-then-write shape. A poll landing in that window returned an empty string and `assert!(content.contains("alpha"))` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2788`) failed. Observed once during M5-03 verification and green on the next 8 runs — a real ordering race, not an environment flake. **The upstream guard the fork dropped:** upstream's helper of the same name and same location, `wait_for_file` in `src/app/input/mod.rs` (v0.8.2 `:900`), wraps the read in `if !content.is_empty() { return content; }` (v0.8.2 `:904`) and has carried it unchanged at every tag from `v0.7.0` through `v0.8.2`. The fork's copy has never had it: `git log --all -S'if !content.is_empty()' -- src/app/input/mod.rs` returns no commit on this branch, so the guard was lost in the initial squashed import (`chore: initial public release`), not by a later edit — it is not an M5 regression. **The fix:** the helper now polls until the file exists AND has non-zero length, under the SAME 2 s deadline and 20 ms interval, and its timeout panic names both the elapsed time and the path (`timed out after {:?} waiting for non-empty {}`) instead of the path alone; a comment records why the guard must not be dropped again. Upstream's extra `#[cfg(unix)]` attribute is deliberately NOT taken back — this fork is Linux-only (ADR 0013). All five callers were audited against the change: every writer emits a non-empty payload (a `cp` of a scrollback holding `alpha\nbeta`, `printf direct`, a three-line `printf`, `printf done`), so none treats an empty file as "ready"; the single discarded result (`let _ = wait_for_file(...)`, `src/app/input/navigate.rs:2718`) uses the call purely as a barrier before the overlay-teardown wait, where blocking until the write completes is strictly stronger. **RED first:** with the pre-fix helper and the editor command temporarily widened to `sh -c 'touch <out>; sleep 0.2; cp "$1" <out>' sh`, so the poll reliably observes the empty file, the target test failed 5/5 — `panicked at src/app/input/navigate.rs:2791: assertion failed: content.contains("alpha")` after 0.171 s, i.e. the helper returned early rather than reaching its 2 s deadline, which is the empty-read path and not a timeout. With the guard applied and that same widened command still in place: 5/5 pass. The temporary widening was then reverted, leaving `src/app/input/navigate.rs` byte-identical to the M5-04 base. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0; the target test 20/20 consecutive; under load `--bin zynk --test-threads 32 -E 'test(/app::/)'` 610/610; `-E 'test(/app::input::navigate/)'` 52/52; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2724/2724 passed, 1 skipped; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). `NOTICE` unchanged — the change is fork-owned test scaffolding with no upstream commit behind it. |
| *(this commit)* | `edd08e89` `c97e0986` `89fe8975` *(all pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Refactor (chain end — typed adapters): the pane-layout, worktree and remaining workspace/tab TUI actions stop hand-rolling `Method` values at the call site and go through typed adapters on `App`, so `src/app/runtime_mutations.rs` is now the ONE place a TUI action becomes an API method.** This row ENDS the chain `1a4e94e5 -> 8b74fb8a -> 97f78226 -> 9a91a2ac -> 0bab0157 -> edd08e89 -> c97e0986 -> 89fe8975` — the three commits are consecutive upstream and the third rewrites what the first two add, so they land as one fork commit and the state here is what the chain is reviewed as. **`edd08e89` (pane layout):** the split/resize/swap/zoom/focus-direction/rename/set-split-ratio call sites in `src/app/input/navigate.rs` and `src/app/input/modal.rs` swap `dispatch_runtime_mutation(id, Method::X(params))` for `runtime_x(id, params)`; `runtime_mutations.rs` gains seven adapters (`runtime_pane_rename`, `runtime_pane_focus_direction`, `runtime_pane_resize`, `runtime_pane_swap`, `runtime_pane_split`, `runtime_pane_zoom`, `runtime_layout_set_split_ratio`). Its behaviour half is the close paths: `close_active_tab_via_api` becomes `close_active_tab_via_api_requires_confirmation() -> bool` and, when the workspace holds its LAST tab, no longer dispatches `tab.close` (which `App::handle_tab_close`, `src/app/api/tabs.rs:228-233`, rejects with `tab_close_failed` "cannot close the last tab in a workspace", so the keypress silently did nothing) but either raises the implicit-worktree-group confirmation (`AppState::confirm_implicit_worktree_group_close`, `src/app/actions.rs:1750`) or closes the workspace through `close_workspace_idx_via_api`; `close_focused_pane_via_api` becomes `close_focused_pane_via_api_requires_confirmation() -> bool`, returning `self.state.mode == Mode::ConfirmClose` so its callers stop tearing the modal/navigate mode down over a confirmation the API just raised (`App::handle_pane_close` answers `confirmation_required`, `src/app/api/panes.rs:1447-1455`). With the TUI actions routed, the inline `AppState`/`Workspace` mutators they used to call have no production caller left and take `#[cfg(test)]` rather than an `allow(dead_code)`: 17 in `src/app/actions.rs` (`switch_tab`, `next`/`previous_workspace`, `next`/`previous_tab`, `next`/`previous_agent`, `focus_agent_entry`, `cycle_agent_entry`, `navigate_pane`, `swap_pane`, `cycle_pane`, `last_pane`, `toggle_zoom`, `close_focused_pane_would_close_workspace`, `close_pane`, `close_tab`) plus its now test-only `find_in_direction`/`NavDirection` import, `AppState::split_pane` and the `Direction` import (`src/app/input/mod.rs`), `focus_toast_target` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs`), `handle_navigate_reserved_key`, the free `handle_navigate_key` and `execute_navigate_action_in_context` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs`), `logging::tab_closed`, four `Workspace` methods (`close_active_tab`, `split_focused`, `close_focused`, `close_active_tab_and_report`) and `Tab::close_focused`. **`c97e0986` (worktrees):** mechanical — the three `src/app/worktrees.rs` submit paths take `runtime_worktree_create_deferred` / `runtime_worktree_open` / `runtime_worktree_remove_deferred`. **`89fe8975` (typed adapters):** the stringly dispatch `9a91a2ac` left behind — workspace create/rename/move, tab create/rename/move and `server.reload_config` in `src/app/input/{modal,navigate}.rs` and the three `App::tick` request flags in `src/app/mod.rs` — becomes six more adapters plus `runtime_server_reload_config`. **Chain end state:** `src/app/runtime_mutations.rs` holds 23 `runtime_*` adapters over the two dispatch primitives (`dispatch_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_api_request`, `dispatch_deferred_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_deferred_api_request`); every one has at least one production caller and ZERO `dispatch_runtime_mutation` / `dispatch_deferred_runtime_mutation` call sites remain outside that file (`grep -rn 'dispatch_\(deferred_\)\?runtime_mutation' src | grep -v runtime_mutations.rs` is empty). Callers are `src/app/input/navigate.rs` (workspace create/move/focus/close, tab create/move/focus/close, pane focus/focus-direction/swap/split/zoom/close, layout set-split-ratio, server reload-config), `src/app/input/modal.rs` (workspace rename, tab create/rename, pane rename/resize/swap and the two close paths), `src/app/mod.rs` (the three `tick` request flags) and `src/app/worktrees.rs` (the three worktree submits). The adapter block is byte-identical to upstream `89fe8975:src/app/runtime_mutations.rs` lines 1-175. **Two direct state mutations deliberately remain** in these files, both identical to upstream at `89fe8975`: `self.state.switch_workspace_tab(ws_idx, tab_idx)` in `App::spawn_overlay_argv_command` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:1003`, upstream `:1019`) — an overlay-pane spawn that already owns the runtime it just built, not a keybind action — and `self.state.close_selected_workspace()` in `App::handle_worktree_remove_finished` (`src/app/worktrees.rs:833`, upstream `:920`) — a deferred git completion callback that arrives outside any API request. **Adaptations beyond a rename:** (1) the fork's `WorkspaceCreateParams`, `TabCreateParams` and `PaneSplitParams` have NO `env` field (upstream's per-pane env is a later v0.8.2 feature this port has not reached), so every ported literal drops upstream's `env: Default::default()`; (2) the `(ContextMenuKind::Tab, "Close")` arm in `src/app/input/modal.rs` takes only the call rename — upstream's pre-image also carries a `leave_modal(&mut self.state)` from `974a4817` "fix: leave context menu after closing tab", which is scheduled for **M5-08**, so wrapping a `leave_modal` the fork does not yet have would import that fix early; (3) upstream's new test helpers hardcode the upstream product name in their `WorktreeSpaceMembership` fixtures — renamed to `zynk` per ADR 0010 §7, matching the helper `src/app/input/navigate.rs` already had; (4) the free `handle_navigate_key` loses its `#[allow(dead_code)]` comment to `#[cfg(test)]`, which is the stronger statement the fork's dead-code rule asks for. **Tests (RED first):** the six upstream tests were added on the M5-04b base first — `tui_close_tab_last_parent_group_workspace_opens_confirmation_via_api` and `tui_close_pane_last_parent_group_pane_opens_confirmation_via_api` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs`), `api_context_menu_close_tab_last_parent_group_workspace_keeps_confirmation_mode` and `api_context_menu_enter_close_pane_last_parent_group_pane_keeps_confirmation_mode` (`src/app/input/modal.rs`), `clicking_pane_context_menu_close_leaves_context_menu_mode` and `clicking_pane_context_menu_close_last_parent_group_pane_keeps_confirmation_mode` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs`) — and 5 of the 6 failed on that base (`left: ContextMenu, right: ConfirmClose` for the modal tab close; `left: 1, right: 0` on `selected` for the navigate tab close; the same shape for the other three). The sixth, `clicking_pane_context_menu_close_leaves_context_menu_mode`, passes on the base by design: it is upstream's regression guard that the NON-confirmation click path still lands in `Mode::Terminal` once the close call becomes conditional. Two fork-only tests extend the M5-03 purity test in `src/app/runtime_mutations.rs`: `runtime_pane_layout_adapters_drive_pure_state_without_a_pty` drives `runtime_pane_split` (unknown target -> structured `pane_not_found`), `runtime_pane_zoom` (toggles `tabs[0].zoomed`) and `runtime_pane_close` (2 panes -> 1) and `runtime_worktree_adapters_dispatch_typed_params_without_a_pty` drives `runtime_worktree_open` and the deferred `runtime_worktree_remove_deferred` (both -> `workspace_not_found`), each asserting `terminal_runtimes.len() == 0` throughout so a PTY spawn would fail the test; both were RED on the base as 8 compile errors (4 `E0599` missing `runtime_*` methods, 4 `E0422` unimported param structs). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*` / `TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and the production-only `cargo clippy --locked -- -D warnings` both exit 0 with zero dead-code warnings from the re-routing; `-E 'test(/input::/) or test(/worktrees/) or test(/runtime_mutations/) or test(/layout/)'` 468/468; `test(/api::schema/)` 32/32; `test(/zynk::header/)` 28/28; `test(/zynk::receipt/)` 29/29; `test(/app::/)` 618/618; `method_wire_ids_are_stable` + `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` 2/2; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test multi_client --test cross_area --test client_mode` 53/53; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2732/2732 passed, 1 skipped (2724 at the base, +8 = 6 upstream + 2 fork-only); `just check` 3050/3050 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks).
